# RE correspondence + parity matrix

> Consolidated from `notes/correspondence.md` (grill sessions 2026-06-01 → 2026-06-10) on 2026-06-10.

Binary: retail `Jointops.exe` (Joint Operations: Combined Arms), imagebase `0x400000`,
IDB `Jointops.exe.kong.i64`. All addresses are absolute in that image. Per-row status:
matching | divergent | unknown, as of the cited grill.

One page for two questions: **which original function does our X correspond to**, and
**how verified is that correspondence**. The per-domain RE records own the format
layouts, divergence catalogs (`D-…` ids), and verdict rationale; this page joins down
into them.

## 1. Per-system parity matrix

Systems whose verdict tables already live in a tracked RE doc are not repeated here —
the row links to the authoritative record.

## 1.1 Control-register animation function table (grilled 2026-07-29)

The numeric style byte is dispatched independently by each consumer; there is
no engine-wide rule that types 113–117 all read a control register.

| Reimplementation | Original | Witnessed contract | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| `threedi_ctrl_register_name` / `threedi_ctrl_register_ordinal` | 96 populated descriptors at `0x83DCE8`; `[orig: CtrlName_ToOrdinal @ 0x57B290]` | exact ordinal order and ASCII case-insensitive lookup; ordinary port API distinguishes an unknown name from valid ordinal 0 | matching |
| `threedi_ctrl_register_loader_ordinal` | `[orig: sub_5B4640 @ 0x5B4640; ordinal store @ 0x5B46E6]` | retail's loader passes a non-null inline name and stores zero for `LOD_FRAC`, unknown, and empty names; OpenNova additionally returns zero for a null API argument as a safety extension | matching retail inputs, compatibility alias isolated to loader API |
| model CTRL-reference conversion | `[orig: ThreediGp_LoadFromFile @ 0x5B5C80..0x5B5DA2; @ 0x5B5E0B..0x5B5EF6; @ 0x5B5F4F..0x5B5F62]` | material/texture animation, PANM, and light file-local references are patched through the model CTRL record to global ordinals | matching semantics |
| `threedi_panm_sample_track_raw` | `[orig: PANM_SampleTrack @ 0x5b2270]` | called by `[orig: Model_TransformBoneMatrices @ 0x58e390]`; type 113 reads the even control value, 114–117 call `wave_lookup` | matching deterministic dispatch/math; partial whole-process RNG lifetime/order (D-3DI-2) |
| `panm_wave_lookup` / `renderer::uv_anim_wave_lookup` | `[orig: wave_lookup @ 0x5de6b0]` | low-nibble dispatch over the shared 2,816-byte waveform table | matching table/dispatch math; partial whole-process RNG lifetime/order (D-3DI-2) |
| `renderer::uv_anim_transform` + live shader rows | `[orig: compute_uv_transform_matrix @ 0x5b1990]` | UV 113=set, 114=scroll/add, 115=shear, 116=scale, 117=rotation; complete row-vector 2×3 matrix | matching and live |
| `renderer::eval_material_runtime` (RGB) | `[orig: RgbGen_EvaluateColor @ 0x5b23d0; low product @ 0x5B24AC]` | type 24 constant; 113/114 signed controlled interpolation; 115–117 waveform fallback; wrapping `IMUL`/`SAR 16` | matching and live |
| `renderer::eval_material_runtime` (alpha) | `[orig: AlphaGen_EvaluateValue @ 0x5b2320; low product @ 0x5B234C]` | type 24 constant; 113 signed controlled interpolation; 114–117 waveform fallback; wrapping `IMUL`/`SAR 16` | matching and live |
| `renderer::compute_anim_frame` | `[orig: apply_shader_parameters @ 0x58db80]` | signed even-dword input; static-zero odd state selects the fractional branch; low-product wrap and exact `0x10000` endpoint retained | matching |
| `renderer::eval_light_runtime` + `NovaObjectData::evaluate_lights` | `[orig: Light_TickGenBlock @ 0x5a8ae0; Light_GetPointLightParams @ 0x5a9180]` | point-light color uses the RGB generator, hence only 113/114 are controlled | matching and live |
| PLAYPARTANIM phase publication | `[orig: Entity_ApplyCommand case 0x22 @ 0x43B192; integrator @ 0x456710; HUD_CacheEntityDisplayInfo @ 0x4A3E18..0x4A3E38]` | channel 1 → `VEHICLE_SPECIAL1` (71) iff item attrib `0x1000` is clear; channel 2 → `VEHICLE_SPECIAL2` (72); wrapping signed ADD/SUB, strict overshoot clamps, raw dword publication; no model-order/blacklist policy | matching and live |

## 5.6 Single-player listen-server bring-up (engine-research, 2026-06-16)

Witness that JO single player is an in-process listen server (host + client, socketless transport
mode 1). Originals only — none reimplemented yet; these gate a future in-process listen-server
(single-player) host. Findings landed in
[net/novaworld-net-re.md §5.0](net/novaworld-net-re.md).

| original | addr | role | evidence | status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SinglePlayer_StartMission` | `0x561af0` | SP launch: `SetConnectionMode(3)` + `SetTransportMode(1)` + `CreateSession` → "Game Loop" | server_name `"SINGLEPLAYERGAME"`; decompile | confirm-only (not reimplemented) |
| `CGameSession_SetConnectionMode` | `0x4c49f0` | conn mode → `is_authority`/`is_mp_session_peer` (1=host, 2=client, 3=host+client) | switch writes +0x5C/+0x60/+0x64 | confirm-only |
| `CNapiNetwork_SetTransportMode` | `0x4c8750` | socket-state field; opens a UDP socket only for modes 2/3/4 (SP=1 → no socket) | `OpenTransportSocket` gate | confirm-only |

The old D.1 `JoinerSession` path has since been consolidated into `npruntime::JoinerConnection` +
`ClientRuntime`, with `NovaSimulation` retaining only the socket/world adapter. Current real-UDP and
two-instance coverage includes the complete initial entity batches, named self-identification, deployed
C2S 0x0C/S2C 0x0A play, redeployment, and authoritative mount relationship confirmation. Remaining
render/motor fidelity is tracked by the current D-INF/D-AI rows rather than the superseded 2026-06-23
“host player only / NPC motor” snapshot.

The primary leg then requires that the embedded MountSlot at parent `+0x2B4` is the live `EquippedSlot` and its Def has a resolved `fpModel` pointer at `+0x16C`; the alternate `Def.flags2(+0x0C) & 0x800` (Invisible) leg forces the cull without the FP-model/equipped-slot comparison. A failed predicate falls through to the normal submit | decompile + disasm 2026-07-20; D-WPN-5; `nova_simulation_test.gd` + `mission_present_pass_test.gd` + `wire_present_pass_test.gd` + `game_world_test.gd` + `mission_runtime_test.gd` | ported as the transient packed `PF_LOCAL_VIEW_SUPPRESSED` verdict: only the exact parent's world-model presentation is hidden, while authoritative `PF_HIDDEN`, entity/collision/simulation state, and separately rendered attached actors remain independent. The host publishes both successful FP gun resolution and the owning weapon-Def identity: a same-Def parent swap reuses the resolved model, while a different Def cannot inherit it. Authored-but-missing `gfx1` data therefore cannot hide the only visible gun, and a valid Def with no `gfx1` does not substitute the bring-up AK. Rebuilding and presenting the verdict every host frame (including zero-fixed-tick frames) restores the model before slot commit, in third person, and immediately on detach |

## 5.7 Per-frame client net role (P5, 2026-06-27; net-re §5.44)

The client counterpart of `Server_TickUpdate`. Witnessed + ported into `libs/npruntime`
(`np::ClientRuntime`) / `libs/netsim`. Verified by `npruntime_client_runtime` (always-on in-process
round-trip + host-as-client) and `npruntime_golden_client` (C2S 0x0C inner + framing byte-parity vs the
gameplay capture; S2C anchor cross-check). Findings landed in [net/novaworld-net-re.md §5.44](net/novaworld-net-re.md).

| original | addr | role | evidence | status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Client_ProcessNetworkFrame` | `0x42c180` | per-frame client net role: recv-pump (`0x42c228`) → raw-input pack (`0x42c3e9`) → C2S 0x0C (`0x42c482`, gated `is_in_session && !is_authority && !dword_81474C && !g_spawn_success_gate`) → send-pump (`0x42c4bc`); NOT authority-gated at its call site (`Game_ProcessMainFrame @0x526692`) | decompile; §5.44 | MATCHING (behavioral proof) |
| `CNapiNetwork_PumpClientProtocolRecv` | `0x4c4fe0` | client recv pump → `NapiNPProtocol_Pump(np, flags=26, 250)` (drain+dispatch S2C) | decompile; §5.44 | confirm-only |
| `CNapiNetwork_PumpClientProtocolSend` | `0x4c5000` | client send pump → `NapiNPProtocol_Pump(np, flags=738, 250)` (flush queued outbound) | decompile; §5.44 | confirm-only |
| `CNapiNetwork_QueueReliableMessage` | `0x4c4fa0` | queues a message on `napi_conn`; drops its `flags` arg → `CNapiNPConnection_QueueMessage(…, msg_flags=0, …, 1024)` | decompile; §5.44 | confirm-only |
| `NapiNPMessage_Create` | `0x627fc0` | builds the outgoing message; `len_field_size` = 3 (LEN8 inner-flag `0x20`) for payload 1..255 / 4 (LEN16) for >255, `msg_flags=0` ⇒ no SKIP bytes — the 0x0C inner-message byte format | decompile; §5.44 | MATCHING (byte-parity: `npruntime_golden_client`) |
| `g_spawn_success_gate` | `0x24c1928` | deploy gate: SET on death/spectator (0x0A `flags1&0x01`) + spawn-select (0x1D), CLEARED on deploy → `!gate` = deployed/alive (the 0x0C send gate) | xrefs; §5.44 / §5.2 / §5.9 | confirm-only |

Reimpl seam (opennova ↔ opennova self-consistent + retail byte-parity where cited):

| reimpl | original anchor | role |
|---|---|---|
| `np::ClientRuntime` / `Client_ProcessNetworkFrame` | `Client_ProcessNetworkFrame @0x42c180` | role-aware headless client (Joiner + host-as-client); recv-fold → C2S 0x0C (`!is_authority`-gated) |
| `np::apply_in_match_c2s` (D-NET-126) | `NapiNPServerMsg_0x00C @0x501c30` | production `PeerC2SInMatch` consumer → `deliver_c2s` onto the owning connection → `Server_TickUpdate` drain |
| `np::is_in_match(conn)` (D-NET-121/122) | `NapiNPServer_SendFiltered @0x4c87e0` | single in-match predicate shared by the drain + emit fan; host loopback no longer starved, anchors to its player |
| `netsim::NetClientView::apply` / `ISessionTransport::deliver_c2s` | `NapiNPProtocol_Pump @0x62a650` | remote-wire fold path / uniform C2S inbound-inject |

## 5.9 Host-initiated session close + the anti-cheat challenge pair (live retail capture 2026-07-26; net-re §5.64/§5.65)

The punt a stock host sends to close a session (the connection-description record, full tag
`0x103`) and the two challenges it streams continuously at a connected client. Reimpl:
`libs/npwire` (`parse_disconnect_event`, `decode_loadout_crc_request`), `libs/npruntime`
(`JoinerConnection::on_host_disconnect` + the `0x20`/`0x21` replies), `NovaSimulation::is_session_lost`,
`NovaDeployScreenPresenter`, `main_game._abort_to_menu`. ctest `npruntime_host_punt` +
`nw_message_coverage`; GUT `godot/tests/net/host_punt_surfacing_test.gd`.

| original | addr | role | evidence | status |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| `Server_TickUpdate` (AFK arm) | `0x51e109` / `0x51e13a` / `0x51e13d` | the SIX-MINUTE idle kick: `GetTickCount() − slot[+0x188E4] > 0x57E40` (360000 ms) → `mismatchType 35` punt — retail's AFK timeout, fired at a player parked on the deploy screen | disasm 2026-07-26; §5.64 | confirm-only (host-side emit unported — D-NET-182) |

## 6. Host Command wiring ([ADR 0001](adr/0001-mnu-action-command-boundary.md), matches)

`UI_DispatchScreenEvent @ 0x54e6a0`, `UI_ShowPreGameMenuByState @ 0x568d10`,
`UI_RegisterOptionsCallbacks @ 0x55d610`, `Expansion_SwitchTo @ 0x5688c0`,
`Expansion_ReloadAllAssets @ 0x568370`.

## 7. Reading the tables

- **addr** is the join key — names drift across IDB passes, addresses don't.
- MNU tag literals are UTF-16 wide; to re-locate one after an IDB rebuild, search
  ASCII-interleaved-with-0x00 bytes or re-anchor via `make_signature_for_function`.
